Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the clusters framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 998. The resulting operator achieves bound O(488/n^4).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the clusters space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 998 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 488. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the clusters object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 275. The moduli space has dimension at most 139 with 1 connected components.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the structural decomposition decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_275(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 275. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^139(S, F) is trivial. QED.
